  OBJ15: Give Lalanoah's Remains to Hamah
  OBJ20: Wait outside the temple (1 hour)
  OBJ30: Talk to Hamah
  OBJ40: (Optional) Devour the Blood Curse of Lalanoah
  OBJ41: (Optional) Return the Blood Curse to Hamah
  OBJ50: Talk to Hamah
  OBJ70: Descend beneath the temple
  OBJ80: Talk to Hamah
  OBJ90: Enter the coffin
  ALIASES: 0=Hamah[UA 5F96B2:Glenmoril.esm]; 1=WaitMarker[REF 5F96E3:Glenmoril.esm]; 2=Embryo; 3=SealMarker[REF 5F96B1:Glenmoril.esm]; 4=CoffinMarker[REF 5F9731:Glenmoril.esm]
  [S0.0] ;0 start up
      Alias_Hamah.GetActorRef().AddItem(Alias_Embryo.GetRef(), 1, false)
      Alias_Hamah.GetRef().Enable(true)
      Alias_Hamah.TryToEvaluatePackage()
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(0, true, false)
      SetActive(true)
  [S10.0] ;10 Talk with Hamah
  [S15.0] ;15 Choice Give Lalanoah or not
      SetObjectiveCompleted(0, true)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(15, true, false)
  [S20.0] ;20 Hamah Gain Lalanoah
      qKeyItem.SetStage(1)
      SetObjectiveCompleted(15, true)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(20, true, false)
  [S20.1] Quest __temp = (Self as Quest)
      revsub05introquestscript kmyQuest = (__temp as revsub05introquestscript)
      kmyQuest.RegisterHamahTimer()
  [S20.2] ;20-3 Ahzidal Check Again
      if CqAhzidal.GetStageDone(50)
      if !CqAhzidal.GetStageDone(100)
      CqAhzidal.SetStage(100)
      endif
      endif
  [S30.0] ;30 Wait End, Talk with Hamah
      SetObjectiveCompleted(20, true)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(30, true, false)
      Sc01.ForceStart()
  [S40.0] ;40 Choice Eat/Abandone Bloodcures of Lalanoah
      SetObjectiveCompleted(30, true)
      game.GetPlayer().AddItem(Alias_Embryo.GetRef(), 1, false)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(40, true, false)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(41, true, false)
  [S45.0] ;45 Eat Arcane
      SetObjectiveCompleted(40, true)
      SetObjectiveFailed(41, true)
      actor PlayerRef = game.GetPlayer()
      PlayerRef.RemoveSpell(AbLalanoah)
      PlayerRef.RemoveSpell(AbHamah)
      PlayerRef.AddSpell(AbStoryteller, true)
      Alias_Hamah.GetActorRef().AddItem(Alias_Embryo.GetRef(), 1, false)
      gLalanoah.SetValueInt(999)
      gHamah.SetValueInt(999)
      gStoryteller.SetValueInt(1)
      SetStage(50)
  [S45.1] ;45-2
      ISMWhiteOut.Apply(1.0)
      utility.wait(0.6)
      Alias_Hamah.GetActorRef().SetOutfit(OufitNoMask, false)
  [S45.2] Quest __temp = (Self as Quest)
      revqachivementpointscript kmyQuest = (__temp as revqachivementpointscript)
      kmyQuest.ModMnemoli(5.0)
  [S46.0] ;46 Back to Hamah
      SetObjectiveFailed(40, true)
      SetObjectiveCompleted(41, true)
      Alias_Hamah.GetActorRef().AddItem(Alias_Embryo.GetRef(), 1, false)
      gStoryteller.SetValueInt(-1)
      SetStage(50)
  [S46.1] Quest __temp = (Self as Quest)
      revqachivementpointscript kmyQuest = (__temp as revqachivementpointscript)
      kmyQuest.ModHumanhood(5.0)
  [S50.0] ;50 After Choice
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(50, true, false)
  [S60.0] ;60 After Talk with Hamah, Break Floe Scene
      SetObjectiveCompleted(50, true)
      ScSeal.ForceStart()
  [S65.0] ;65
      SpellArtRef.Enable(false)
  [S70.0] ;70 Open Seal
      FloeRef.PlaceAtMe(FloeExp, 1, false, false)
      FloeRef.Disable(false)
      SpellArtRef.Disable(false)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(70, true, false)
  [S80.0] ;80 Hamah Scene
      Alias_Hamah.TryToMoveTo(HamahMarker02)
      Alias_Hamah.TryToEvaluatePackage()
      SetObjectiveCompleted(70, true)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(80, true, false)
      Sc02.ForceStart()
  [S90.0] ;90
      JephreDoor.Enable(false)
      SetObjectiveCompleted(80, true)
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(90, true, false)
      debug.MessageBox("To be Continued. Wait V0.97")
  [S100.0] ;100 In Falmereth
      SetObjectiveCompleted(90, true)
      utility.wait(1.0)
      Stop()
  [S999.0] ;999 Shut Down
      JephreDoor.Enable(false)
      FloeRef.Disable(false)

######## 62F3BC:Glenmoril.esm zzzRevSub06Key01

      KeyTriggerRef.Enable()
      SetObjectiveDisplayed(0)
      SetActive()
  [S10.0] ;10
      gYelemString.SetValue(1)
      KeyTriggerRef.Disable()
      SetObjectiveCompleted(0)
      Game.GetPlayer().AddItem(Alias_YelemString.GetRef())
      Alias_BodyMarker.TryToDisable()
  [S10.1] <no source>
  [S100.0] ;100
      gYelemString.SetValue(999)
      Alias_Container.GetRef().AddItem(Alias_YelemString.GetRef())
      Stop()
  [S999.0] ;999
      Alias_Container.TryToDisable()
